- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Isabella, leader of Spain (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Spain
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Conquistador, Replacing Explorer (Requires technology: Compass)
        - Unique Unit: Inquisition, Replacing Inquisitor (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Unit: Armada, Replacing Corvette (Requires technology: Navigation)
        - Unique Building: Bullring, Replacing Zoo (Requires technology: Scientific Theory)
        - Unique Improvement: Hacienda (Cannot be built adjacent to another Hacienda. Gains additional yields for each adjacent Resource:
          +3 Food from Bonus Resources
          +3 Production from Strategic Resources
          +3 Gold from Luxury Resources); Requires technology: Steel
        - Civilization Ability: +10 Gold and +4 Faith from gaining tiles, scaling with Era. Newly founded Cities adopt the Majority Religion of your Capital. May purchase Naval Units with Faith.
      - Leader: Isabella
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Radar: Allows you to build the Heavy Bomber, a deadly air Unit capable of delivering death from afar, and the Fighter, an air Unit designed to wrest control of the skies from enemy aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Advanced Ballistics, Mobile Tactics
      - Refrigeration: Allows Work Boats to construct an Offshore Platform, and increases Trade Route maximum travel distance.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics, Computers
      
      ## Policies
      - Co-Prosperity Sphere (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Co-Prosperity Sphere: Bullying City-States no longer reduces Influence, revokes Quests, or cancels Pledges of Protection. Doing so decreases the Influence of all other Civs with the bullied City-State by 10%, and increases your Influence by 10%.
      - Total War (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Total War: +25% Production when building Land Units. Warscore increases 25% more quickly, and it is 25% easier to bully City-States.
      - Air Supremacy (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Air Supremacy: Receive a free Airport in every City. +25% Production when building Aircraft. Can build Zeroes.
      - Cult of Personality (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Cult of Personality: +50% Tourism to civilizations fighting a common enemy. 50% of your highest Warscore counts as a Tourism Modifier with all Civilizations. Free Great Person of your choice.
      - Lightning Warfare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lightning Warfare: +3 Movement for Great Generals. Gunpowder units gain +15% attack and ignore enemy ZOC; Armored units gain +15% attack and +1 Movement.
      - Police State (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Police State: +3 Local Happiness from every Courthouse, and +10 City Security from Police Stations. +100% Production towards Courthouses and Police Stations.
      - Mare Nostrum (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Mare Nostrum: +1 Oil and Coal for every City-State Alliance. +2 Production and Culture to Atolls, Fishing Boats, and Offshore Oil Platforms. Naval Ranged Units gain the Mare Nostrum Promotion.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Regimental System (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Earn Great Generals and Great Admirals 33% faster.
        - * Great Generals and Great Admirals gain the Regimental Traditions Promotion (+10% Leadership Combat Bonus and +1 Tile Radius).
        - * +2 additional copies of the unique Luxury Resources from expending Great Admirals for their Voyage of Discovery ability.
        - * Military Units can be upgraded in territory owned by Vassals and friendly City-States.
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Equality (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness in every City and +1 Happiness for every 15 Citizens in a City.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from and Distress, Poverty, Illiteracy, and Boredom in all Cities.
      - Organization (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Production in every City.
        - * +25% Production toward Workers and Trade Units.
        - * +1 Movement for Workers.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - Militarism (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Culture in Cities with at least 10 Strength.
        - * -15% Unit Maintenance.
        - * -50% Route Maintenance.
      - Justice (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - * Cities with a garrison gain +25% Ranged Combat Strength.
        - * Royal Guardhouse built in Capital (+3 Production, +2 Defense, +50 Hit Points, +10% Military Supply from Population in all Cities, 1 Engineer Slot).
      - Serfdom (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Culture and Faith in Cities for every 4 non-Specialist Citizens.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from Pastures.
      - Entrepreneurship (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from Workshops.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from every Mine, Quarry, and Lumber Mill.
        - * +25% Yields when you expend Great Merchants or Great Engineers for their Instant Yield abilities.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Foreign Service (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 100 Spy Points.
        - * Earn Great Diplomats 50% faster.
        - * +50% Rewards from City-State Quests.
        - * +1 of every Strategic Resource for every three City-State Alliances you maintain.
      - Fraternity (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +3 Food in every City.
        - * +3 Science from City Connections.
      - Organized Religion (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +25% Pressure from owned Cities following your Primary Religion.
        - * +1 Delegate in the World Congress for every 10 Cities following your Primary Religion.
        - * +2 Culture from Shrines and Temples.
      - Divine Right (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +33% Yields from Internal Trade Routes.
        - * Completing an Internal Trade Route triggers a Historic Event as if completing an International Trade Route.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Syndicalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Syndicalism: Corporate Franchises count double in the Cities of Civilizations you are at least Popular with, and this extra Franchise does not count against your Global Franchise maximum. Can establish 5 additional Franchises in foreign Cities. +2 Production from all Corporation Offices.

    - #0:
      - role: user
      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 6
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 2
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 9
        - Boldness: 3
        - WarBias: 4
        - HostileBias: 7

        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 4

        - MinorCivWarBias: 4
        - DenounceWillingness: 5
        - Forgiveness: 4
        - Meanness: 7
        - Neediness: 9
        - Chattiness: 8
        - DeceptiveBias: 7
        - Rationale: Tweaked by In-Game AI (Turn 421: Adjusted wartime persona for maximum survival. MinorCivCompetitiveness increased to 9 (desperate for Harappa oil). Boldness reduced to 3 (defensive survival mode). GuardedBias at 9 for extreme caution. Neediness at 9 for maximum alliance-seeking with Carthage. DeceptiveBias at 7 allows wartime flexibility and potential surprise nuclear escalation. HostileBias/Meanness increased for deterrence against Brazil/Indonesia.)
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Refrigeration
        - Rationale: Turn 421: Nuclear Fission still 11 turns away. Radar should complete in ~6 turns unlocking Heavy Bombers/Fighters critical for air defense. Refrigeration is the logical follow-up, leading toward Advanced Ballistics and Computers. Need continuous tech progress for military capabilities and eventually nuclear delivery systems.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Total War (Policy)
        - Rationale: Turn 421: Total War (Autocracy Level 2) remains essential. +25% Production for Land Units is critical given my 3,217 military vs Brazil's 11,750 and Indonesia's 5,746. With Raiatea under siege and potential multi-front war, unit production speed is survival. Warscore bonus also valuable for future peace negotiations once nuclear deterrent is ready.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Zurich
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Abandoning Zurich influence campaign. Polynesia's 242 influence is insurmountable without massive emissary investment that would detract from winnable city-states. Zurich's Ivory quest is unfulfillable (no Ivory in our territory per resources list). Redirecting diplomatic resources to Harappa and Eraclea where we have competitive positions (43 and 34 influence respectively) and can securequest completions.
        - UpdatedTurn: 150
        
        ### Kwa-Bulawayo
        - Public: 80
        - Private: 75
        - Rationale: Turn 282: Increasing Kwa-Bulawayo priority from 60/55 to 80/75. Panormus capture imminent (97% health) will trigger 120 influence quest reward. This could flip our -53 influence significantly. Polynesia has 360 influence but quest completion plus reward could make this competitive. Positioning for post-capture influence surge.
        - UpdatedTurn: 283
        
        ### Msoura
        - Public: 80
        - Private: 75
        - Rationale: Increasing Kwa-Bulawayo priority. Panormus capture by our forces appears imminent based on previous tactical reports. Quest completion could provide 120 influence reward, potentially flipping our -48 position. Polynesia leads with 332 influence but quest surge could make this competitive. Positioning for post-capture influence gains.
        - UpdatedTurn: 287
        
        ### Nippur
        - Public: 70
        - Private: 65
        - Rationale: Turn 331: Upgrading Nippur relationship to 70/65. Spy operations and natural influence decay have improved our position to 79 influence (Friends status). Portugal leads with 736 but this city-state provides diplomatic diversification. Maintaining friendship for potential future alliance opportunities.
        - UpdatedTurn: 332
        
        ### Eraclea
        - Public: 30
        - Private: 25
        - Rationale: Turn 344: Maintaining Eraclea relationship at reduced priority (previously higher). Portugal's spy coup reduced our influence to 219 (Friends) vs their 652 (Ally). Trade route still generates value but unlikely to win back immediately. Maintaining channels for future spy operations or diplomatic shifts when resources permit. Not abandoning but recognizing current limitations while focusing on winnable city-states like Harappa.
        - UpdatedTurn: 344
        
        ### Melbourne
        - Public: 85
        - Private: 80
        - Rationale: Turn 400: Melbourne has Buddhism, 257 influence (Friends), and is my only viable trade route partner under Cold War restrictions. Open Door policy blocks alliance but repeal could happen in future. Must maintain dominant position here for economic survival (-325 GPT crisis). Has Porcelain and Lapis Lazuli. Cannot afford to lose this relationship.
        - UpdatedTurn: 401
        
        ### China
        - Public: -10
        - Private: -5
        - Rationale: Turn 414: China has Order ideology with Brazil/Indonesia but isn't at war with me. Slight negative reflects ideological opposition and their denunciation, but not actively hostile to avoid three-front war. They may support Decolonization since Indonesia proposed it.
        - UpdatedTurn: 415
        
        ### Assyria
        - Public: 85
        - Private: 80
        - Rationale: Turn 416: Adjusting Assyria to 85/80. They remain a Portugal vassal with limited military (2043 strength), but we just renewed our Ivory-for-GPT deal with them. Maintaining positive relations for diplomatic credibility, though acknowledging they cannot provide meaningful military aid against Brazil.
        - UpdatedTurn: 416
        
        ### Portugal
        - Public: 50
        - Private: 45
        - Rationale: Turn 420: Adjusting Portugal relationship to 50/45 (slight increase). Peace treaty expires in 1 turn. Portugal has 7,703 military, fighting Brazil (common enemy), and follows Order ideology. They have massive city-state control (26 delegates). While they maintain spy operations against me, potential for limited cooperation against Brazil exists once treaty expires. Slightly positive stance to explore options without full commitment.
        - UpdatedTurn: 420
        
        ### Carthage
        - Public: 95
        - Private: 90
        - Rationale: Turn 421: MAXIMUM Carthage relationship maintained at 95/90. They just upgraded to Heavy Bomber and won Harappa election (+210 influence). Carthage has 35,575 military, Atomic Bomb, Apollo Program complete - they are my ONLY hope for survival. Both follow Autocracy and Buddhism, both at war with Brazil. Their nuclear coordination is existential for me. Events show them actively producing advanced units.
        - UpdatedTurn: 422
        
        ### Indonesia
        - Public: -90
        - Private: -85
        - Rationale: Turn 421: Increasing Indonesia hostility to -90/-85. They continue devastating assault on Raiatea (now 44% health) with tanks, artillery, Heavy Bombers, and newly upgraded Fighter. Their Decolonization proposal threatens Harappa oil access. At war with 5,746 military vs my weakened forces. They just created Great Merchant showing continued economic-military mobilization. Maximum hostility reflects existential threat.
        - UpdatedTurn: 422
        
        ### Brazil
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -95
        - Rationale: Turn 421: Brazil at MAXIMUM hostility -100/-95. They completed Apollo Program (science victory progress), have 3 Heavy Bombers + 3 Fighters devastating my forces, 11,750 military strength, and are at 285% cultural influence on me (Dominant). They have cultural victory ready - just need Citizen Earth Protocol. Created Great Musician this turn. Existential threat requiring maximum enmity.
        - UpdatedTurn: 422
        
        ### Polynesia
        - Public: 25
        - Private: 20
        - Rationale: Turn 421: Reducing Polynesia relationship to 25/20 (from 35/30). They have Declaration of Friendship with me but follow Order ideology with Brazil/Indonesia/China. In World War scenario, their loyalty to Order may override our friendship. Events show them maintaining neutral/observing stance. Must be realistic about their limited reliability while preserving basic friendship for trade route access under Cold War.
        - UpdatedTurn: 422
        
        ### Harappa
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 95
        - Rationale: Turn 421: CRITICAL - Harappa city-state now at MAXIMUM priority 100/95. Carthage just won election gaining 210 influence (now 780, Ally). My spy Esmeralda failed election rigging. I have 149 influence (Friends). Harappa has OIL - I pay 56 GPT to Brazil for oil I desperately need for mechanized units. Decolonization proposal could reset this. Must secure alliance through any means - emissaries, quests, or waiting for Carthage to lose influence naturally.
        - UpdatedTurn: 422
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: Turn 421: CRITICAL DEFENSE MODE. Raiatea at 44% health under Indonesian heavy assault with tanks/bombers. Nuclear Fission 11 turns away - must survive. Defense/CityDefense/Mobilization at 100 for maximum survival capacity. Air/Antiair at 100 to counter Brazil's 3 Heavy Bombers + 3 Fighters and Indonesia's air attacks. Nuke/UseNuke at 100/80 to prepare immediate nuclear deterrent upon tech completion. Production 100 essential for unit replacement. Diplomacy 85 to maintain Carthage alliance - my only nuclear-armed ally with 35k military. Espionage 80 to retry Harappa oil-critical city-state. Abandon all expansion/wonder/culture priorities.
        - GrandStrategy: Spaceship
        
        ### Flavors

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 44
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 6 turns
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - World Religion: Confucianism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Indonesian Sphere of Influence: Cahokia:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 360
          - Natural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each Natural Wonder provides +2 to all Yields when worked.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cultural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each World Wonder provides +2 Culture to its city, and each Great Work provides +1 Science to its city.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Indonesian Sphere of Influence: Marrakech:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 382
          - Open Door: Melbourne:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Portuguese Sphere of Influence: Sogut:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Portuguese Sphere of Influence: Msoura:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 415
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Historical Landmarks:
            - Description: Great Person tile improvements provide +1 to Food, Production, and Gold when worked, and Landmarks built by Archaeologists provide +1 Science, Culture, and Faith when worked.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Archaeology.
            - EnactedOn: 371
          - World Ideology: Order:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - EnactedOn: 404
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Sanction: Carthage:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: 371
          - Cold War:
            - Description: Ideological differences have pushed the world into a cold war, and economic and political factions are forming based on ideology alone.
              While this resolution is active, players may only make Trade Routes with civilizations that follow the same ideology, or with allied City-States. Furthermore, Influence levels with allied City-States do not decline.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - EnactedOn: 393

        ### 60
        - Type: PlayerDoneTurn

        - Player: 2: Spain
        
        You, Isabella (leader of Spain, Player 2), are making strategic decisions after turn 422.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Isabella, leader of Spain (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Spain
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Conquistador, Replacing Explorer (Requires technology: Compass)
        - Unique Unit: Inquisition, Replacing Inquisitor (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Unit: Armada, Replacing Corvette (Requires technology: Navigation)
        - Unique Building: Bullring, Replacing Zoo (Requires technology: Scientific Theory)
        - Unique Improvement: Hacienda (Cannot be built adjacent to another Hacienda. Gains additional yields for each adjacent Resource:
          +3 Food from Bonus Resources
          +3 Production from Strategic Resources
          +3 Gold from Luxury Resources); Requires technology: Steel
        - Civilization Ability: +10 Gold and +4 Faith from gaining tiles, scaling with Era. Newly founded Cities adopt the Majority Religion of your Capital. May purchase Naval Units with Faith.
      - Leader: Isabella
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Radar: Allows you to build the Heavy Bomber, a deadly air Unit capable of delivering death from afar, and the Fighter, an air Unit designed to wrest control of the skies from enemy aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Advanced Ballistics, Mobile Tactics
      - Refrigeration: Allows Work Boats to construct an Offshore Platform, and increases Trade Route maximum travel distance.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics, Computers
      
      ## Policies
      - Co-Prosperity Sphere (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Co-Prosperity Sphere: Bullying City-States no longer reduces Influence, revokes Quests, or cancels Pledges of Protection. Doing so decreases the Influence of all other Civs with the bullied City-State by 10%, and increases your Influence by 10%.
      - Total War (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Total War: +25% Production when building Land Units. Warscore increases 25% more quickly, and it is 25% easier to bully City-States.
      - Air Supremacy (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Air Supremacy: Receive a free Airport in every City. +25% Production when building Aircraft. Can build Zeroes.
      - Cult of Personality (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Cult of Personality: +50% Tourism to civilizations fighting a common enemy. 50% of your highest Warscore counts as a Tourism Modifier with all Civilizations. Free Great Person of your choice.
      - Lightning Warfare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lightning Warfare: +3 Movement for Great Generals. Gunpowder units gain +15% attack and ignore enemy ZOC; Armored units gain +15% attack and +1 Movement.
      - Police State (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Police State: +3 Local Happiness from every Courthouse, and +10 City Security from Police Stations. +100% Production towards Courthouses and Police Stations.
      - Mare Nostrum (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Mare Nostrum: +1 Oil and Coal for every City-State Alliance. +2 Production and Culture to Atolls, Fishing Boats, and Offshore Oil Platforms. Naval Ranged Units gain the Mare Nostrum Promotion.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Regimental System (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Earn Great Generals and Great Admirals 33% faster.
        - * Great Generals and Great Admirals gain the Regimental Traditions Promotion (+10% Leadership Combat Bonus and +1 Tile Radius).
        - * +2 additional copies of the unique Luxury Resources from expending Great Admirals for their Voyage of Discovery ability.
        - * Military Units can be upgraded in territory owned by Vassals and friendly City-States.
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Equality (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness in every City and +1 Happiness for every 15 Citizens in a City.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from and Distress, Poverty, Illiteracy, and Boredom in all Cities.
      - Organization (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Production in every City.
        - * +25% Production toward Workers and Trade Units.
        - * +1 Movement for Workers.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - Militarism (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Culture in Cities with at least 10 Strength.
        - * -15% Unit Maintenance.
        - * -50% Route Maintenance.
      - Justice (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - * Cities with a garrison gain +25% Ranged Combat Strength.
        - * Royal Guardhouse built in Capital (+3 Production, +2 Defense, +50 Hit Points, +10% Military Supply from Population in all Cities, 1 Engineer Slot).
      - Serfdom (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Culture and Faith in Cities for every 4 non-Specialist Citizens.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from Pastures.
      - Entrepreneurship (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from Workshops.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from every Mine, Quarry, and Lumber Mill.
        - * +25% Yields when you expend Great Merchants or Great Engineers for their Instant Yield abilities.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Foreign Service (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 100 Spy Points.
        - * Earn Great Diplomats 50% faster.
        - * +50% Rewards from City-State Quests.
        - * +1 of every Strategic Resource for every three City-State Alliances you maintain.
      - Fraternity (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +3 Food in every City.
        - * +3 Science from City Connections.
      - Organized Religion (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +25% Pressure from owned Cities following your Primary Religion.
        - * +1 Delegate in the World Congress for every 10 Cities following your Primary Religion.
        - * +2 Culture from Shrines and Temples.
      - Divine Right (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +33% Yields from Internal Trade Routes.
        - * Completing an Internal Trade Route triggers a Historic Event as if completing an International Trade Route.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Syndicalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Syndicalism: Corporate Franchises count double in the Cities of Civilizations you are at least Popular with, and this extra Franchise does not count against your Global Franchise maximum. Can establish 5 additional Franchises in foreign Cities. +2 Production from all Corporation Offices.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Assessing Grand Strategy**
            
            I'm currently evaluating strategic options to determine the most effective grand strategy. My focus is on identifying the path that best leverages our strengths and advances our objectives within the game's current state.
            
            **Refining Strategic Direction**
            
            My current focus is on reassessing our grand strategy, particularly in light of Brazil's looming cultural victory. I'm analyzing whether our current Spaceship focus remains viable or if a pivot is necessary to counter their dominance.
            
            **Analyzing Victory Conditions**
            
            I've discovered Brazil is dangerously close to a cultural victory, having influenced seven civilizations and adopted Order policies. Simultaneously, Indonesia presents a similar threat. My current focus is assessing the immediate danger from both, especially with our ongoing war on two fronts.
            
            **Defending Territories**
            
            I'm currently assessing our military situation, focusing on the besieged city of Raiatea which is heavily outnumbered by Indonesian forces. While our other land zones appear secure against Spanish incursions, Raiatea demands immediate attention to prevent its loss.
            
            **Evaluating Naval Superiority**
            
            I'm now assessing our naval strength post-engagement. Despite losing a destroyer, our fleet remains formidable, having sunk an enemy battleship and their Great Admiral. This naval dominance appears to be holding the Indonesian threat at bay for now.
            
            **Prioritizing Research Pathways**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our research trajectory. While Refrigeration has its merits for infrastructure, the immediate need for Radar to bolster our defenses and offensive capabilities against two active superpowers takes precedence. I'm now confirming Radar as our next technological pursuit.
            
            **Choosing Autocracy Ideals**
            
            I'm currently prioritizing the selection of Autocracy ideals. My focus is on integrating higher-level ones like Air Supremacy and Cult of Personality, while considering how to best synergize them with ongoing research and existing policy branches.
            
            **Confirming Policy Selection**
            
            I am now confirming the exact nomenclature for implementing the "Total War" tenet within the Autocracy branch. My current focus is ensuring the correct string is used to apply the policy, specifically addressing the "Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy" designation to avoid any implementation errors.
            
            **Confirming Policy Selection**
            
            I am now confirming the exact nomenclature for implementing the "Total War" tenet within the Autocracy branch. My current focus is ensuring the correct string is used to apply the policy, specifically addressing the "Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy" designation to avoid any implementation errors.
            
            **Analyzing Grand Strategy**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our grand strategy of "Spaceship" (Science Victory) given our current technological standing. We are notably behind the leading civilizations. Simultaneously, Brazil is on the cusp of a Cultural Victory with 7 influential civilizations, which requires immediate strategic consideration.
            
            **Disrupting Victory Paths**
            
            I'm now focused on Brazil's cultural victory progress. While they've met the ideology and influence requirements, the crucial Citizen Earth Protocol wonder remains their final hurdle. I'm assessing if our ongoing war can disrupt their capital or if direct intervention is needed to halt construction.
            
            **Assessing Threat Vectors**
            
            I'm now re-evaluating Brazil's proximity and potential threat to our core territories. Their current position relative to our cities suggests a more immediate concern than initially assumed, especially with their puppet states bordering our besieged Raiatea.
            
            **Defending Core Territories**
            
            I'm now assessing our core cities' defenses and the significant paratrooper presence within them. This robust airborne force offers a viable strategic option to fall back and secure our heartland from the immediate Indonesian threat near Raiatea.
            
            **Leveraging Alliances**
            
            I'm now assessing our strategic position and recent intel. Carthage, our powerful, friendly neighbor, is at war with Brazil, a primary threat. This alliance is incredibly fortuitous, especially considering Assyria's capitulated state under Portugal, whom Carthage has also denounced.
            
            **Disrupting Victory Vectors**
            
            My current focus is disrupting Brazil's path to victory. With Carthage already pressuring them, I'm exploring options to sabotage their production of the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder, potentially using our spy network in Rio de Janeiro to achieve this objective and stall their progress.
            
            **Gathering Intelligence**
            
            I am currently gathering intelligence on Carthage and assessing our financial situation. My focus is on understanding our gold deficit and whether purchasing units with faith is a viable option given our tight economy.
            
            **Selecting Ideology Policies**
            
            I am now reviewing available ideology policies to identify the most impactful choices. My immediate focus is on implementing "Foreign Service" and "Trade Confederacy" from the Statecraft branch to bolster our diplomatic and economic capabilities.
            
            **Selecting Ideology Tenets**
            
            I'm now reviewing the Autocracy tenets again, focusing on their synergy with our current war effort. While Total War was a prior selection, I'm now prioritizing Air Supremacy and Cult of Personality for their significant benefits in air unit production and tourism generation against common enemies.
            
            **Prioritizing Technologies**
            
            I'm now prioritizing research. While Refrigeration offers infrastructure benefits, the critical need for Radar to enhance our defensive and offensive air capabilities against rival superpowers has become evident. Radar is now confirmed as the next technological pursuit.
            
            **Prioritizing Defensive Air Power**
            
            My current focus is securing air superiority. With enemy bombers bombarding Raiatea and our navy, and our only fighter in poor health, acquiring Radar is paramount to upgrading our air forces. This will allow for the development of Fighters and Heavy Bombers to counter these threats.
            
            **Assessing Victory Pathways**
            
            My current assessment reveals we're far from cultural or diplomatic victory. Domination is challenging due to ongoing wars. While our science output is strong, my focus remains on maximizing our "Spaceship" victory potential by prioritizing research and defensive strategies to outlast our opponents.
            
            **Adjusting Tactical Priorities**
            
            My focus is now on fine-tuning our short-term tactical flavors to better support our long-term Spaceship victory goal. I'm adjusting values like Expansion and Growth to reflect our current defensive posture and war footing.
            
            **Addressing Happiness Deficit**
            
            I'm now prioritizing addressing our critical happiness deficit. The current "Unhappy - combat penalty" status is severely hindering our defensive capabilities, particularly around Raiatea and our navy. I'm analyzing the city-specific happiness deltas and potential causes like war weariness and public opinion to formulate a solution.
            
            **Addressing Ideological Conflict**
            
            I'm currently grappling with a significant happiness deficit stemming from an ideological clash. As the sole Autocracy civilization amidst a sea of Order, public opinion is crushing our citizens, severely impacting combat effectiveness. I'm investigating the feasibility of switching ideologies to alleviate this critical issue.
            
            **Seeking Happiness Solutions**
            
            I am now investigating policy options to alleviate our critical happiness deficit, particularly exploring the massive boost offered by Equality in the Progress tree. This seems a more viable solution than relying on staff or revolutions to change ideologies.
            
            **Adopting Equality Policy**
            
            My immediate focus is on alleviating our critical happiness deficit. The "Equality" policy offers a substantial boost, addressing unhappiness from various sources and providing additional happiness per city and citizen. I've concluded that adopting "Equality" is far more critical than "Total War" at this juncture to resolve our combat penalty.
            
            **Adopting Equality Policy**
            
            My immediate focus is on alleviating our critical happiness deficit. The "Equality" policy offers a substantial boost, addressing unhappiness from various sources and providing additional happiness per city and citizen. I've concluded that adopting "Equality" is far more critical than "Total War" at this juncture to resolve our combat penalty.
            
            **Selecting Next Policy**
            
            I've confirmed that "Equality (Continuing Progress Branch)" is selectable as our next policy. This will resolve our critical happiness deficit and remove the debilitating combat penalty we're currently suffering during this two-front war.
            
            **Reviewing Diplomatic Stance**
            
            I am now reviewing our diplomatic relationships, noting positive ties with Carthage and Harappa, while Indonesia and Brazil remain hostile. I'm particularly scrutinizing our relationship with China, noting their significant technological advantage and guarded stance towards us.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Alignments**
            
            I'm reassessing my diplomatic stance regarding Portugal, noting our "untrustworthy" status and mutual denouncements, which contrasts with our strong alliance with Carthage and positive relations with Harappa and Polynesia. I'm evaluating the necessity and feasibility of improving these relationships, especially considering Portugal's diplomatic victory potential.
            
            **Assessing AI Personalities**
            
            I'm now analyzing the AI personas of Brazil and Indonesia. Brazil's high HostileBias and low WorkWithWillingness explain their refusal to make peace, while Indonesia's war score indicates they're winning and unwilling to yield. I need to align my persona to better match their aggressive stances.
            
            **Adjusting Persona Parameters**
            
            I am now refining my AI persona, leaning into my defensive and scientific objectives. I'm adjusting parameters like DenounceWillingness and DeceptiveBias to better reflect our current strategic posture of survival and technological pursuit, while keeping our alliance with Carthage strong.
            
            **Prioritizing AI Flavors**
            
            I am now fine-tuning our AI persona's flavor settings to address critical needs. My immediate focus is on prioritizing happiness at 100 to combat the severe penalties, and gold at 75 due to our current deficit. Defense, city defense, and air capabilities are also set to 100, with science prioritized at 95.
            
            **Prioritizing Happiness First**
            
            I'm now focused on resolving our critical happiness deficit. Adopting the "Equality" policy is paramount to remove combat penalties, bolster defenses, and stabilize our empire, even over immediate military policies.
            
            **Implementing Strategic Adjustments**
            
            I am now confirming the precise implementation of our AI persona's adjustments. With relationships stable, my focus is on setting research to Radar, policies to Equality, and prioritizing flavors for happiness, gold, defense, and science to ensure our immediate survival and long-term victory.
